    Sub LoadData(Process As String, bmid As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)

        Dim token As IntPtr = IntPtr.Zero

        LogonUser(My.Settings.PathUserName, My.Settings.PathServer, My.Settings.PathPassword, 9, 0, token)
        If LogonUser(My.Settings.PathUserName, My.Settings.PathServer, My.Settings.PathPassword, 9, 0, token) = True Then
            Using person As WindowsImpersonationContext = New WindowsIdentity(token).Impersonate()
                Select Case Process
                    Case "DC"
                        DCData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "DB"
                        DBData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "WB"
                        WBData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "MP"
                        MPData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "PL"
                        PLData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "FT"
                        FTData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "FL"
                        FLData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "ATC"
                        ATCData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "SING"
                        SINGData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "TP"
                        TPData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "MEMS"
                        MEMSData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                    Case "EDS"
                        EDSData(bmid, timeofrequest, mcno)
                End Select
            End Using
        End If

        ' EMakingPrint(bmid, Process)
    End Sub
    Private Function RetPFImg(valPF As String) As String
        Select Case valPF.Trim
            Case "Passed"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\PBox2.png"
            Case "Failed"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\FBox2.png"
            Case Else
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\PFNBox2.png"
        End Select
    End Function
    Private Function RetABCImg_2(valABC As String) As String
        Select Case valABC.Trim
            Case "A"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\A.png"
            Case "B"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\B.png"
            Case "C"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\C.png"

        End Select
    End Function
    Private Function RetABCImg(valABC As String) As String
        Select Case valABC.Trim
            Case "A"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\ABox.png"
            Case "B"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\BBox.png"
            Case "C"
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\CBox.png"
            Case Else
                Return "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\images\NBox.png"
        End Select
    End Function
    Private Function getProcCodes(procCodeID As String, tableName As String, procCodeIDCrit As String, retValue As String) As String
        Try

            Dim oConn As New SqlConnection
            Dim pCodes As String = "0"

            oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
            oConn.Open()
            Dim Sql As String = "SELECT " & retValue & " FROM " & tableName & " Where " & procCodeIDCrit & "=" & procCodeID & ""
            Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
            Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)

            Do While DR.Read
                pCodes = DR(retValue).ToString
            Loop
            oConn.Close()
            Return pCodes

        Catch ex As Exception
            Return "0"
        End Try

    End Function
    Private Function getBMWhyInfo(BMWhyID As String, retField As String) As String
        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim mSQLStr As String = "SELECT " & retField & " FROM BMWhy WHERE BMWhyID=" & BMWhyID

        Dim emDC As New SqlCommand(mSQLStr, oConn)
        Dim emDR As SqlDataReader = (emDC.ExecuteReader)
        Dim retData As String = ""

        Do While emDR.Read

            retData = emDR(retField).ToString

        Loop
        emDR.Close()
        Return retData
    End Function


    Private Function getBMMCUnit(BMMCID As String) As String
        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim mSQLStr As String = "SELECT BMMCUnit, BMMCUnitID FROM BMMCUnit where BMMCUnitID = '" & BMMCID & "'"

        Dim emDC As New SqlCommand(mSQLStr, oConn)
        Dim emDR As SqlDataReader = (emDC.ExecuteReader)
        Dim retData As String = ""

        Do While emDR.Read

            retData = emDR("BMMCUnit").ToString

        Loop
        emDR.Close()
        Return retData
    End Function

    Private Sub MEMSData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")
        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\MEMS FT-TPTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection
        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewMEMSData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("D35").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("G35").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("J35").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("P35").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewMEMSData  where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("D36").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("G36").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("J36").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("P36").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause  
            'ws.Cells("W37").Value = DRss2("Why4").ToString  'Cause  
            'History

        'Jameson2023

        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)

        Do While DR.Read
            ws.Cells("X2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString

            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("N8").Value = "✔"

                ws.Cells("Q8").Value = "✔"

            ws.Cells("AD10").Value = DR("LastActBeforeProb").ToString

                ws.Cells("AD13").Value = "Yes"

            ws.Cells("AD14").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreqQuantity").ToString

            ws.Cells("AG14").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreq").ToString

            'ADDED UPON THE REQUEST UPDATE 02/19/2024 CARIGAO

                'wb.Save(serverFilePath)

                Dim downloadsPath As String = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile), "Downloads")
                Dim localFilePath As String = Path.Combine(downloadsPath, fileNamestr)

                'File.Copy(serverFilePath, localFilePath, True)
                wb.Save(localFilePath)

                Try
                    Dim AdobeSoftwares As Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE").OpenSubKey("ADOBE")
                    If AdobeSoftwares Is Nothing Then
                        Me.Close()
                        Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                    Else
                        If Not Array.IndexOf(AdobeSoftwares.GetSubKeyNames, "Acrobat Reader") = -1 Then
                            Me.Show()

                            Dim PLTemp As New WebBrowser
                            PLTemp.Navigate(localFilePath)
                            PLTemp.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                            Me.Controls.Add(PLTemp)


                        Else
                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try




            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub


        Loop

    End Sub
    Private Sub WBData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\WBTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection
        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewWBData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read

            ws.Cells("N39").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W39").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("K39").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'History
            ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewWBData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("N40").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W40").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("K40").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            ws.Cells("G40").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString


            'History
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()





        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewWBData_Ver4 Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DR.Read

            ws.Cells("AH2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AR2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AM2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AW2").Value = DR("GLAndon2").ToString

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("G10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("K10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("O10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("S10").Value = "✔"

            End If

            If getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "MEKA" Then
                ws.Cells("G11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "QUALITY" Then
                ws.Cells("N11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PERIODIC INSPECTION" Then

            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "SETUP" Then

                ws.Cells("AA11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "OTHERS" Then
                ws.Cells("U11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PRE/POST-PROCESS FEEDBACK INVESTIGATION" Then

            'Total Waiting Time/Stop Time
            'ws.Cells("AQ24").Value = DR("TotalStopTime").ToString
            'ws.Cells("AR25").Value = DR("TotalStopTime").ToString 'vanessa
            ws.Cells("AR26").Value = DR("TurnOverTo").ToString 'vanessa

            ws.Cells("H69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'PM Category
            ws.Cells("N69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'BM Content
            ws.Cells("T69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'Case
            ws.Cells("Z69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'Machine Unit
            ws.Cells("AE69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'Position
            ws.Cells("AJ69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'Work Content
            ws.Cells("AO69").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'BM Cause
            ws.Cells("Y72").Style.ShrinkToFit = True 'Preventive Measure



            ws.PrintOptions.BottomMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.LeftMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.TopMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.RightMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.FitToPage = False
            ws.PrintOptions.PaperType = PaperType.A4
            ws.PrintOptions.HorizontalCentered = True
            ws.PrintOptions.VerticalCentered = True
            ws.PrintOptions.Portrait = True


            Dim ProcessCase As String = "WB"
            Dim folderPath As String = "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\TempData\Print Out\" & ProcessCase
            Dim fileNamestr As String = ProcessCase & "_" & bmID & ".pdf"
            Dim serverFilePath As String = Path.Combine(folderPath, fileNamestr)
            Try
                'wb.Save(serverFilePath)

                Dim downloadsPath As String = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile), "Downloads")
                Dim localFilePath As String = Path.Combine(downloadsPath, fileNamestr)

                'File.Copy(serverFilePath, localFilePath, True)
                wb.Save(localFilePath)

                Try
                    Dim AdobeSoftwares As Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE").OpenSubKey("ADOBE")
                    If AdobeSoftwares Is Nothing Then
                        Me.Close()
                        Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                    Else
                        If Not Array.IndexOf(AdobeSoftwares.GetSubKeyNames, "Acrobat Reader") = -1 Then
                            Me.Show()

                            Dim PLTemp As New WebBrowser
                            PLTemp.Navigate(localFilePath)
                            PLTemp.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                            Me.Controls.Add(PLTemp)


                        Else
                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try

            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try


            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop



    End Sub
    Private Sub DBData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\DBTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()


        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewDBData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("G36").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("K36").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("O36").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W36").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewDBData_Ver4  where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("G37").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("K37").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("O37").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W37").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause  
            'ws.Cells("W37").Value = DRss2("Why4").ToString  'Cause  
            'History
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()



        Dim Sql As String = "Select * FROM ViewDBData_Ver4 Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read

            ws.Cells("AG2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AH2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AM2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AR2").Value = DR("GLAndon2").ToString

            ws.Cells("AB7").Value = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First

            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM

            End If

            If getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "MEKA" Then
                ws.Cells("G11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "QUALITY" Then
                ws.Cells("N11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PERIODIC INSPECTION" Then

                            Me.Show()

                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try






            'AxAcroPDF5.CreateControl()

            'AxAcroPDF5.setZoom(100)
            'AxAcroPDF5.setShowToolbar(False)
            'AxAcroPDF5.Show()
            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop


    End Sub
    Private Sub DCData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\DCTemplate2.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()



        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewDCData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("O36").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W36").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("K36").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString.Split("").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("G36").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString

            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewDCData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("O37").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W37").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("K37").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            ws.Cells("G37").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString


            'History
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()



        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewDCData_Ver4 Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)



        Do While DR.Read

            ws.Cells("AD2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AI2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AN2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AS2").Value = DR("GLAndon2").ToString

            ws.Cells("AF8").Value = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AK8").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AP8").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AU8").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmation").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("BF8").Value = DR("EndBM").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("G10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("K10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("O10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("S10").Value = "✔"
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("W10").Value = "✔"
            End If

            If getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "MEKA" Then
                ws.Cells("G11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "QUALITY" Then
                ws.Cells("N11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PERIODIC INSPECTION" Then
                ws.Cells("G12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "CORRECTIVE MAINTENANCE" Then

            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "FEEDBACK INVESTIGATION" Then

            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "SETUP" Then

            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PM DETECTION" Then
                ws.Cells("N12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "OP DETECTION" Then
                ws.Cells("AA11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "OTHERS" Then
                ws.Cells("U11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "PRE/POST-PROCESS FEEDBACK INVESTIGATION" Then
                ws.Cells("U12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If

            ws.Cells("Z20").Value = DR("StartPM1").ToString
            ws.Cells("AF20").Value = DR("StartPM2").ToString
            ws.Cells("Z21").Value = DR("EndPM1").ToString
            ws.Cells("AF21").Value = DR("EndPM2").ToString

            ws.Cells("AD4").Value = DR("RequestedBy").ToString 'OP ID No.
            ws.Cells("AD4").Style.WrapText = True
            ws.Cells("AI4").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            If (DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString = DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString) Then
                ws.Cells("AN4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString()  'PM ID No.
            Else
                ws.Cells("AN4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString() & ", " & DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString()
            End If
            ws.Cells("AS4").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmationBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            ws.Cells("BD4").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.
            ws.Cells("BD62").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.


            If DR("MCStatus").ToString = "" Then
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Stop" Then
                'ws.Cells("AQ16").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AR16").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Running" Then
                'ws.Cells("AZ16").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("BA16").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If

            ws.Cells("AR11").Value = DR("MCNo").ToString 'M/C No
            ws.Cells("AR12").Value = DR("MCType").ToString 'M/CT ype
            ws.Cells("AR13").Value = DR("PkgName").ToString 'PkgName
            ws.Cells("AR15").Value = DR("LOTNo").ToString 'Lot No
            ws.Cells("AR14").Value = DR("DeviceName").ToString 'Device Name
            Dim arrDT As String() = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString().Split(" ").ToArray()
            ws.Cells("AR18").Value = arrDT(1) & " " & arrDT(2) 'Time Requested
            'ws.Cells("AQ20").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToString 'Time Start
            'ws.Cells("AQ21").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToString 'Time Ended
            'JAMESON2023
            Try
                ws.Cells("AR20").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToShortDateString 'Time Start 
                ws.Cells("BB20").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToShortTimeString 'Time Start 
                ws.Cells("AR21").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToShortDateString 'Time Ended
                ws.Cells("BB21").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToShortTimeString 'Time Ended
            Catch ex As Exception

            End Try


            'JAMESON2023
            'ws.Cells("U10").Value = DR("AlarmNo").ToString


            ws.Cells("K13").Value = DR("RequestItem").ToString
            'ws.Cells("U11").Value = Me.getProcCodes(DR("BMUnitID").ToString, "BMMCUnit", "BMMCUnitID", "BMMCUnit") 'DISABLED BY JAMESON
            'ws.Cells("U13").Value = DR("Prioritization").ToString
            ws.Cells("AR17").Value = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString().Split(" ").First()

            ws.Cells("K15").Value = DR("RequestContent").ToString
            'ws.Cells("E23").Value = DR("ConditionAfterAdj").ToString & " " & DR("ConditionAfterAdjMin").ToString & "/" & DR("ConditionAfterAdjMax").ToString
            ws.Cells("AC17").Value = DR("AQI").ToString

            ws.Cells("K17").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreqQuantity").ToString & " / " & DR("OccurrenceFreqLotFrame").ToString
            'ws.Cells("AL13").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreq").ToString

            ws.Cells("G24").Value = DR("Why1")
            ws.Cells("G27").Value = DR("Why2")
            ws.Cells("G30").Value = DR("Why3")
            'ws.Cells("E34").Value = DR("Why3")
            ws.Cells("I33").Value = DR("BMWhy4")
            'ws.Cells("AQ28").Value = DR("AccreditorRemarks").ToString

            'If DR("AccreditorRemarks").ToString <> "" Then
            '    ws.Cells(36, 24).Style.Font.Color = SpreadsheetColor.FromName(ColorName.Red)
            'End If

            'Jameson2023
            ws.Cells("M34").Value = DR("DateWhen").ToString 'Time Edited/Created
            If DR("LotRetrieval").ToString = "Yes" Then
                ws.Cells("AY30").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single

                'wb.Save(serverFilePath)

                Dim downloadsPath As String = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile), "Downloads")
                Dim localFilePath As String = Path.Combine(downloadsPath, fileNamestr)

                'File.Copy(serverFilePath, localFilePath, True)
                wb.Save(localFilePath)

                Try
                    Dim AdobeSoftwares As Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE").OpenSubKey("ADOBE")
                    If AdobeSoftwares Is Nothing Then
                        Me.Close()
                        Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                    Else
                        If Not Array.IndexOf(AdobeSoftwares.GetSubKeyNames, "Acrobat Reader") = -1 Then
                            Me.Show()

                            Dim PLTemp As New WebBrowser
                            PLTemp.Navigate(localFilePath)
                            PLTemp.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                            Me.Controls.Add(PLTemp)


                        Else
                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try





            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop

    End Sub
    Private Sub MPData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\MPTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()


        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewMPData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("I29").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("O29").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("F29").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString.Split("").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("C29").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString

            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewMPData_Ver4 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)

                            Me.Show()

                        End If

                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try

            'AxAcroPDF5.setZoom(100)
            'AxAcroPDF5.setShowToolbar(False)
            'AxAcroPDF5.Show()
            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop



    End Sub
    Private Sub PLData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\PLTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()


        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewPLData_Ver2 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("E40").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("I40").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("N40").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("AA40").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewPLData_Ver2  where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("E41").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("I41").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("N41").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("AA41").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause  
            'History
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()


        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewPLData_Ver2 Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read
            ws.Cells("AG2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AM2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AS2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AY2").Value = DR("GLAndon2")

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("J11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("J11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("N11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("N11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("R11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("R11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "4" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("V11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("V11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "8" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("AA11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AA11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("AI11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AI11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If

            ws.Cells("AG5").Value = DR("RequestedBy").ToString 'OP ID No.
            ws.Cells("AG5").Style.WrapText = True
            ws.Cells("AM5").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            If (DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString = DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString) Then
                ws.Cells("AS5").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString()  'PM ID No.
            Else
                ws.Cells("AS5").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString() & ", " & DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString()
            End If
            ws.Cells("AY5").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmationBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            ws.Cells("BL5").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.

            ws.Cells("BD12").Value = DR("MCNo").ToString 'M/C No
            ws.Cells("BD11").Value = DR("MCType").ToString 'M/CT ype        
            ws.Cells("BD14").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToString 'Time Start
            ws.Cells("BD13").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToString 'Time Ended 

            ws.Cells("AA23").Value = DR("LotNo1").ToString
            ws.Cells("AA24").Value = DR("LotNo2").ToString
            ws.Cells("AJ23").Value = DR("LotNo3").ToString
            ws.Cells("AJ24").Value = DR("LotNo4").ToString

            'ws.Cells("K14").Value = ddlRequestItem.SelectedItem.Text
            ws.Cells("K16").Value = DR("HowCN").ToString
            ws.Cells("K18").Value = DR("WhyCN").ToString

            If (DR("TRC").ToString = "Yes") Then
                ws.Cells("Q23").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            Else
                ws.Cells("S23").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If

                'wb.Save(serverFilePath)

                Dim localFilePath As String = Path.Combine(downloadsPath, fileNamestr)

                'File.Copy(serverFilePath, localFilePath, True)
                wb.Save(localFilePath)

                Try
                    Dim AdobeSoftwares As Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE").OpenSubKey("ADOBE")
                    If AdobeSoftwares Is Nothing Then
                        Me.Close()
                        Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                    Else
                        If Not Array.IndexOf(AdobeSoftwares.GetSubKeyNames, "Acrobat Reader") = -1 Then
                            Me.Show()

                            Dim PLTemp As New WebBrowser
                            PLTemp.Navigate(localFilePath)
                            PLTemp.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                            Me.Controls.Add(PLTemp)


                        Else
                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try




            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop

    End Sub

    Private Sub FTData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\FTTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewFTData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)




        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History


            ws.Cells("C39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("F39").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("I39").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("O39").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 

            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString.Split("").ToArray().First


            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewFTData  where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("C40").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("F40").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("I40").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("O40").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause  

            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date



            'History
        Loop
        DRss2.Close()

        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewFTData Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read
            ws.Cells("U2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("Y2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AC2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG2").Value = DR("GLAndon2")

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("E8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("H8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("K8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("N8").Value = "✔"
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("Q8").Value = "✔"
            End If

            ' ws.Cells("AE10").Value = tbxLastActBeforeProb.Text
            ws.Cells("AE13").Value = DR("AlarmNo").ToString
            'If DR("AQI").ToString = "With" Then
            '	ws.Cells("AD13").Value = "Yes"
            'Else
            '	ws.Cells("AD13").Value = "No"
            'End If
            ws.Cells("AE12").Value = DR("AQI").ToString
            ws.Cells("AE15").Value = Me.getProcCodes(DR("BMUnitID").ToString, "BMMCUnit", "BMMCUnitID", "BMMCUnit")
            ws.Cells("AE14").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreqQuantity").ToString & "/" & DR("OccurrenceFreqLotFrame").ToString
            ws.Cells("AH14").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreq").ToString

            ws.Cells("AE16").Value = DR("MCStatus").ToString
            ' ws.Cells("AE17").Value = ddlLeaderChk.SelectedValue
            ' ws.Cells("AE18").Value = tbxLineLoc.Text

            ' Users Account
            ws.Cells("U4").Value = DR("RequestedBy").ToString 'OP ID No.
            ws.Cells("Y4").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            If (DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString = DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString) Then
                ws.Cells("AC4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString()  'PM ID No.
            Else
                ws.Cells("AC4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString() & ", " & DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString()
            End If
            ws.Cells("AG4").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmationBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            ws.Cells("AP4").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.
            ws.Cells("AN68").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.


            ws.Cells("AN13").Value = DR("MCNo").ToString 'M/C No
            ws.Cells("AN12").Value = DR("MCType").ToString 'M/CT ype

                ws.Cells("T56").Value = arrData1(3)

            'jameson2023

                ws.Cells("AJ36").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single

                If DR("Remarks") <> "" Then
                    ws.Cells("AB38").Value = DR("Remarks").ToString
                End If
                If DR("LotRetrievalConfirm") <> "" Then
                    ws.Cells("AP38").Value = DR("LotRetrievalConfirm").ToString
                End If
                If DR("LotRetrievalDate") <> "" Then
                    ws.Cells("AP36").Value = Format(CDate(DR("LotRetrievalDate").ToString), "yy.MM.dd").ToString()
                End If



                'ws.Cells("BD32").Value = DR("LotRetrievalDate").ToShortDateString 'Time Start 
            ElseIf DR("LotRetrieval").ToString = "No" Then
                ws.Cells("AM36").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AM36").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If
            'jameson2023

            ws.Cells("AG26").Value = DR("TurnOverTo").ToString

            ws.Cells("AG27").Value = DR("LotNoForInspection").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG28").Value = DR("PackageForInspection").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG29").Value = DR("DeviceforInspection").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG30").Value = DR("ProdTreatOccStatus").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG32").Value = DR("ProdTreatLotTreat").ToString
            'ws.Cells("AF30").Value = tbxProdTreatTRC.Text
            ws.Cells("AB37").Value = DR("ProdTreatInspResult").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG31").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight

            'ws.Cells("AE35").Value = tbxRPMBefore1.Text

                            ws.Cells("T46").Value = arrAfterTreatment(i)
                        End If

                    End If
                Next

    Private Function getMCNamebyID(MCID As String)
        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection
        Dim MCType As String = ""

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand("GetProcCodes", oConn)
        cmd.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure

        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT Top 1 a.BMMCUnit FROM BMMCUnit a inner join MCType b on(a.MCTypeID=b.MCTypeID) WHERE BMMCUnitID = '" & MCID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read
            MCType = DR("BMMCUnit").ToString
        Loop
        oConn.Close()

        Return MCType
    End Function
    Private Sub SINGData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\SINGTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim remarksData(0) As String
        Dim imagesData(0) As String

        imagesData(0) = ""
        Dim iCnt As Integer = 0


        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewSINGData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DRss1.Read
            ws.Cells("G36").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString 'Date
            ws.Cells("K36").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("O36").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W36").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewSINGData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            ws.Cells("G37").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString 'Date 
            ws.Cells("K37").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("O37").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W37").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()

        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewSINGData Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)

        Do While DR.Read

            ws.Cells("AD2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AI2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AN2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AS2").Value = DR("GLAndon2").ToString

            'Reese 10/28/2024
            ws.Cells("AF8").Value = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AK8").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AP8").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqDate").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("AU8").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmation").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
            'ws.Cells("BF8").Value = DR("EndBM").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().Firstws.Cells("BF8").Value = DR("EndBM").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First


            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("G10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("K10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("O10").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("S10").Value = "✔"
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("W10").Value = "✔"
            End If

            If getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "(PM0) PERIODIC INSPECTION" Then
                ws.Cells("G11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("G11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "(PM1) RESTORATION" Then
                ws.Cells("O11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("O11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "(PM2) QC FEEDBACK" Then
                ws.Cells("W11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("W11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "(PM3) OP NG DISCOVERY" Then
                ws.Cells("AE11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("AE11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "(PM4) KANAGATA PERIODIC INSPECTION" Then
                ws.Cells("G12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("G12").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "MEKA/ELECTRICAL" Then
                ws.Cells("O12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("O12").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "OTHERS" Then
                ws.Cells("W12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("W12").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            ElseIf getProcCodes(DR("BMContentID").ToString, "BMContent", "BMContentID", "BMContent") = "QUALITY" Then
                ws.Cells("AE12").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                'ws.Cells("AE12").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight 'Reese 10/31/2024
            End If



            ws.Cells("AD4").Value = DR("RequestedBy").ToString 'OP ID No.
            ws.Cells("AD4").Style.WrapText = True
            ws.Cells("AI4").Value = DR("GLConfirmOPReqBy").ToString 'GL ID No.  
            If (DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString = DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString) Then
                ws.Cells("AN4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString()  'PM ID No.  
            Else
                ws.Cells("AN4").Value = DR("PMConfirmGLOPReqBy").ToString() & ", " & DR("RepairedByEnd").ToString()
            End If
            ws.Cells("AS4").Value = DR("GLRepairConfirmationBy").ToString 'GL ID No.
            'Jovie Nov 5, 2024 - Show PMI/Accreditor ONLY if either of Akapen1-3 is selected
            If DR("Akapen1").ToString <> "0" AndAlso DR("Akapen1").ToString <> "" Or
               DR("Akapen2").ToString <> "0" AndAlso DR("Akapen2").ToString <> "" Or
               DR("Akapen3").ToString <> "0" AndAlso DR("Akapen3").ToString <> "" Then
                ws.Cells("BE6").Value = DR("EndBM").ToString.Split(" ").ToArray().First
                ws.Cells("BE7").Value = DR("EndBMBy").ToString 'PM ID No.
            End If

            Dim cellStyle = ws.Cells("BB16").Style  'Reese 10/28/2024
            If DR("MCStatus").ToString = "" Then
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Stop" Then
                ws.Cells("AR16").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AR16").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Running" Then
                ws.Cells("AW16").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("AW16").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single

                'Reese 10/28/2024
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "EMGStop" Then
                ws.Cells("BB16").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("BB16").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
                cellStyle.FillPattern.SetSolid(Color.Red)
            End If

            ws.Cells("AR11").Value = DR("MCNo").ToString 'M/C No 
            ws.Cells("AR12").Value = DR("MCType").ToString 'M/CT ype
            ws.Cells("AR13").Value = DR("PkgName").ToString 'PkgName
            ws.Cells("AR14").Value = DR("DeviceName").ToString 'Device Name
            ws.Cells("AR15").Value = DR("LOTNo").ToString 'Lot No

            Dim arrDT As String() = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString().Split(" ").ToArray()


            ws.Cells("AR18").Value = arrDT(1) & " " & arrDT(2) 'Time Requested  
            ws.Cells("AR20").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToShortDateString 'Time Start 
            ws.Cells("BB20").Value = DR("StartOfRepair").ToShortTimeString 'Time Start 
            ws.Cells("AR21").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToShortDateString 'Time Ended
            ws.Cells("BB21").Value = DR("EndOfRepair").ToShortTimeString 'Time Ended

            ws.Cells("K13").Value = DR("RequestItem").ToString
            ws.Cells("AR17").Value = DR("OccurrenceDate").ToString().Split(" ").First()

            ws.Cells("K15").Value = DR("RequestContent").ToString

            ws.Cells("AC17").Value = DR("AQI").ToString

                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try

            'AxAcroPDF5.setShowToolbar(False)
            'AxAcroPDF5.Show()
            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub

        Loop

    Private Sub FLData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\FLTemplate2.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()



        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewFLData_V2 where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History


            ws.Cells("G36").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString
            ws.Cells("K36").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No 
            ws.Cells("O36").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("W36").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 

            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString.Split("").ToArray().First


            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewFLData_V2  where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("G37").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString

            'AxAcroPDF5.Show()

    Private Sub TPData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\TPTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""
        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()


        Dim Sqlss1 As String = "SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ViewTPData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss1 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss1, oConn)
        Dim DRss1 = (DCss1.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss1.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("I39").Value = DRss1("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("N39").Value = DRss1("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("F39").Value = DRss1("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString.Split("").ToArray().First
            ws.Cells("C39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString

            'History
        Loop

        DRss1.Close()

        Dim Sqlss2 As String = "SELECT TOP 2 * FROM ViewTPData where mcno ='" & mcno & "' and bmid <> '" & bmID & "' and TimeOfRequest < '" & timeofrequest & "' ORDER BY bmID DESC"
        Dim DCss2 As New SqlCommand(Sqlss2, oConn)
        Dim DRss2 = (DCss2.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DRss2.Read
            'History
            ws.Cells("I40").Value = DRss2("BMCase").ToString  'Case 
            ws.Cells("N40").Value = DRss2("BMWhy4").ToString  'Cause 
            ws.Cells("F40").Value = DRss2("RepairedByEnd").ToString   'Employee No  
            'ws.Cells("G39").Value = DRss1("TimeOfRequest").ToString 'Date
            ws.Cells("C40").Value = DRss2("TimeOfRequest").ToShortDateString


            'History
        Loop

        DRss2.Close()


        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewTPData Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read
            ws.Cells("T2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("X2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AB2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AF2").Value = DR("GLAndon2")

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("D8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("G8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("J8").Value = "✔"
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("M8").Value = "✔"
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("P8").Value = "✔"
            End If

            ws.Cells("AD10").Value = DR("LastActBeforeProb").ToString

            If DR("AQI").ToString = "With" Then
                ws.Cells("AD14").Value = "Yes"
            Else
                ws.Cells("AD14").Value = "No"
            End If

            ws.Cells("AD15").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreqQuantity").ToString
            ws.Cells("AF15").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreqLotFrame").ToString
            ws.Cells("AG15").Value = DR("OccurrenceFreq").ToString

            ws.Cells("AD16").Value = DR("MCStatus").ToString
            ws.Cells("AD17").Value = DR("LeaderChk").ToString
            ws.Cells("AD18").Value = DR("LineLoc").ToString

            'If DR("NGDetectionCircumtances").ToString = "Alarm Occurs" Then
            '    ws.Cells("K11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
            '    ws.Cells("K11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            'ElseIf DR("NGDetectionCircumtances").ToString = "BM Occurs" Then
            '    ws.Cells("O11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
            '    ws.Cells("O11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            'ElseIf DR("NGDetectionCircumtances").ToString = "Ninshiki NG" Then
            '    ws.Cells("S11").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
            '    ws.Cells("S11").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            'ElseIf DR("NGDetectionCircumtances").ToString = "OP Inspection" Then

                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If

                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try

            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try

            'AxAcroPDF5.setZoom(100)
            'AxAcroPDF5.setShowToolbar(False)
            'AxAcroPDF5.Show()
            oConn.Close()
            Exit Sub
        Loop



    End Sub
    Private Sub EDSData(bmID As Integer, timeofrequest As String, mcno As String)
        SpreadsheetInfo.SetLicense("FREE-LIMITED-KEY")

        Dim wb As ExcelFile = ExcelFile.Load("\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\Templates\EDSTemplate.xlsx")
        Dim ws As ExcelWorksheet = wb.Worksheets.ActiveWorksheet

        Dim Filename As String = ""

        Dim oConn As New SqlConnection

        oConn = New SqlConnection(My.Settings.DBConnection)
        oConn.Open()

        Dim Sql As String = "SELECT * FROM ViewEDSData Where bmID ='" & bmID & "'"
        Dim DC As New SqlCommand(Sql, oConn)
        Dim DR = (DC.ExecuteReader)


        Do While DR.Read

            ws.Cells("W2").Value = DR("Andon").ToString
            ws.Cells("Z2").Value = DR("GLAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AE2").Value = DR("PMAndon").ToString
            ws.Cells("AJ2").Value = DR("GLAndon2")

            If DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "1" Then 'BM
                ws.Cells("E8").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("E8").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'PM
                ws.Cells("H8").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("H8").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "3" Then 'CM
                ws.Cells("K8").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("K8").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("PMCategoryID").ToString = "2" Then 'Set-up/TC
                ws.Cells("N8").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("N8").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            Else 'Others
                ws.Cells("Q8").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("Q8").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            End If

            'ADDED UPON THE REQUEST UPDATE 02/19/2024 CARIGAO
            Dim cellStyle = ws.Cells("J10").Style
            If DR("MCStatus").ToString = "" Then
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Stop" Then
                ws.Cells("G10").Style.Font.Weight = ExcelFont.BoldWeight
                ws.Cells("G10").Style.Font.UnderlineStyle = UnderlineStyle.Single
            ElseIf DR("MCStatus").ToString = "Running" Then

            ws.Cells("X76").Value = DR("AoPen3").ToString
            ws.Cells("AQ45").Value = DR("CheckItem").ToString

            ws.Cells("AM46").Value = DR("ReqTo").ToString

            ws.Cells("AT47").Value = DR("Rate").ToString
            ws.Cells("AM47").Value = DR("Result").ToString
            'Jameson2023


            ws.PrintOptions.BottomMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.LeftMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.TopMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.RightMargin = (0)
            ws.PrintOptions.FitToPage = False
            ws.PrintOptions.PaperType = PaperType.A4
            ws.PrintOptions.HorizontalCentered = True
            ws.PrintOptions.VerticalCentered = True
            ws.PrintOptions.Portrait = True


            Dim ProcessCase As String = "EDS"
            Dim folderPath As String = "\\" & My.Settings.PathServer & "\eMaintenanceSystem\TempData\Print Out\" & ProcessCase
            Dim fileNamestr As String = ProcessCase & "_" & bmID & ".pdf"
            Dim serverFilePath As String = Path.Combine(folderPath, fileNamestr)
            Try

                'wb.Save(serverFilePath)

                Dim downloadsPath As String = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile), "Downloads")
                Dim localFilePath As String = Path.Combine(downloadsPath, fileNamestr)

                'File.Copy(serverFilePath, localFilePath, True)
                wb.Save(localFilePath)

                Try
                    Dim AdobeSoftwares As Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKey = Microsoft.Win32.Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE").OpenSubKey("ADOBE")
                    If AdobeSoftwares Is Nothing Then
                        Me.Close()
                        Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                    Else
                        If Not Array.IndexOf(AdobeSoftwares.GetSubKeyNames, "Acrobat Reader") = -1 Then
                            Me.Show()

                            Dim PLTemp As New WebBrowser
                            PLTemp.Navigate(localFilePath)
                            PLTemp.Dock = DockStyle.Fill
                            Me.Controls.Add(PLTemp)


                        Else
                            Me.Close()
                            Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)

                        End If
                    End If
                Catch ex As Exception
                    Me.Close()
                    Process.Start("iexplore.exe", localFilePath)
                End Try
            Catch ex As Exception
                MsgBox(ex.ToString, MsgBoxStyle.Information, "Production Endgineering Systems")
                Exit Sub
            End Try





        Loop
        oConn.Close()


    End Sub


    Private Sub TabControl1_DrawItem(sender As Object, e As DrawItemEventArgs)
        Dim tabctl As TabControl = DirectCast(sender, TabControl)
        Dim g As Graphics = e.Graphics
        Dim font As Font = tabctl.Font
        Dim brush As New SolidBrush(Color.Black)
        Dim tabTextArea As RectangleF = RectangleF.op_Implicit(tabctl.GetTabRect(e.Index))

        If tabctl.SelectedIndex = e.Index Then
            font = New Font(font, FontStyle.Bold)
            brush = New SolidBrush(Color.White)
            g.FillRectangle(Brushes.LimeGreen, tabTextArea)
        End If
        g.DrawString(tabctl.TabPages(e.Index).Text, font, brush, tabTextArea)
    End Sub

    Private Sub Form3_Load(sender As Object, e As EventArgs) Handles MyBase.Load

    End Sub
End Class
